# Score

Status: generated: `python3 tools/decomp.py score` rewrites the block between the markers
Last updated: 2026-10-22
Summary: how many of the retail game's functions this tree matches byte for byte, which, and which it leaves out.

The score counts unique retail function addresses and their recorded extents, not C++ symbols: linker-folded aliases
count once. `build/matched.tsv` keeps a row for each matched symbol, so its line count can exceed the function count.
Function matches do not by themselves validate initialized globals, a linked executable, or player-visible behaviour;
those need their own evidence. Independently identified library entries constrain caller relocations. Remaining
unpinned external placements are consistency inferences; agreement between reconstructed callers alone does not prove
a library identity.

Function names are those of `config/functions.tsv`: the name of the symbol each function's source compiles to, in the
table's `Class__Method` form (`config/README.md`). Addresses and sizes are measured from the retail executable.

This tree leaves out five of the sources retail was built from (the README's "What is left out"), so the score below is
the one it reproduces. The 33 functions those sources define are listed after it, under "Not in this tree"; they are
not counted as matched here.

<!-- score:begin -->
**5,449 of 5,482 game functions (99.40%) and 1,359,136 of 1,367,287 bytes (99.40%) match the retail executable byte for byte.** 1 matched through a build concession, bytes written into the compiled object after compiling (`config/object-patches.tsv`; the source alone leaves 5,448 matching). 5,449 functions are annotated. The 1,185 compiler-generated unwind funclets (19,169 bytes) are counted with their parent objects once those match. 32 import thunks (the linker's `jmp [IAT]` stubs for DLL calls, each verified against the import table) are library code and not counted.
